# PHP 5.6: verification of Jamalofski/php-src-security@61cd630

Generated by `.github/scripts/verify_backports.py`. Branch `eol-fixes-5.6-security`, pinned at [61cd630](https://github.com/Jamalofski/php-src-security/commit/61cd630a9156112cba47ec8dbb6a63bf671b9856), compared with php/php-src.

Base: [05fb101](https://github.com/Jamalofski/php-src-security/commit/05fb10165081cb4fb55a9943b44cab62c627db7a) has the tree of the official tag [php-5.6.40](https://github.com/php/php-src/releases/tag/php-5.6.40), apart from NEWS, configure.in, main/php_version.h.

337 commits: 90 adapted, 46 clean merge, 52 identical, 26 identical code, NEWS/tests differ, 3 merge with changes, 31 no official commit, 79 no official commit, NEWS/tests only, 10 same changes, context differs.

| 1 | [1633657](https://github.com/Jamalofski/php-src-security/commit/1633657cb6a20b24e69c5a008089eb21ec027d06) | - | backport needed changes for OpenSSL 1.1 | - | no official commit | Cherry-pick (same changed lines) of Remi Collet's 4ca75ff, head of remicollet/php-src PHP-5.6-openssl11, committed here by Anatol Belski on 2019-01-21: a 5.6 port of the ext/openssl part of php-src's OpenSSL 1.1.0 support (Jakub Zelenka's openssl_1_1_port, merged for 7.1 and backported to 7.0.19 as cdc33251d2), without that commit's compatibility layer for OpenSSL < 1.1, so the branch only builds against 1.1 and later. Each converted function matches cdc33251d2 / php-7.0.33 apart from the PHP 5 zval and TSRMLS API: EVP_MD_CTX and EVP_CIPHER_CTX allocated with EVP_MD_CTX_create()/EVP_CIPHER_CTX_new() and freed on every path (openssl_sign, openssl_verify, openssl_digest, openssl_seal, openssl_open, openssl_encrypt, openssl_decrypt, phar_verify_signature), RSA/DSA/DH/EC read through EVP_PKEY_get0_*() and the *_get0_*() accessors without frees, openssl_pkey_new() building keys with RSA/DSA/DH *_set0_*() on freshly allocated BIGNUMs (OPENSSL_PKEY_SET_BN sets NULL for an absent field), X509_EXTENSION_get_data(), X509_NAME_oneline() and X509_get_signature_nid() in openssl_x509_parse(), OPENSSL_ALGO_DSS1 dropped, already-known OIDs skipped in add_oid_section(), SSLv2 removed from xp_ssl.c, and the same test updates (sni_server.phpt is XFAIL where 7.0.33 skips it on 1.1). The Windows-only win_cert_verify_callback() still reads x509_store_ctx->cert here and is converted later by #30 84d9fde, and the ext/phar/util.c hunk (identical to the 7.0 port) is under PHAR_HAVE_OPENSSL, which this build does not define (configure: 'Checking for library libeay32st.lib ... <not found>', 'Could not enable native OpenSSL support in Phar'), so phar signs and verifies through openssl_sign()/openssl_verify(). No double free, use after free, set0 on a borrowed object or uninitialised read was found, the BIGNUM leaks on the error paths of php_openssl_pkey_init_*() are those of php-src, and nothing in the commit depends on timing or heap layout, so it cannot explain the intermittent phar_setsignaturealgo2.phpt failure. Two parts of the 7.0 port are missing: the openssl_csr_get_public_key() fix and the server ECDH guard in xp_ssl.c. Defect (checked in the code): without php-src e840071 ('Never return private part of the key from openssl_csr_get_public_key'), openssl_csr_get_public_key() returns X509_REQ_get_pubkey(csr) as is; under OpenSSL 1.1 that is the EVP_PKEY cached by X509_REQ_set_pubkey(), so for a CSR made by openssl_csr_new() in the same request the returned key holds the private key, which openssl_pkey_export() writes out. e840071 duplicates the request with X509_REQ_dup() first. Remark, pre-existing in php-5.6.40 and not from this commit: phar_call_openssl_signverify() in ext/phar/util.c, the path these builds use, reads Z_BVAL_P(retval_ptr) after efree(retval_ptr) when the call returns a bool, which only openssl_sign() does, so signing a phar with Phar::OPENSSL fails at random (verification takes the IS_LONG branch, which reads the value first); this is the intermittent phar_setsignaturealgo2.phpt failure. |

| 82 | [2c6ec30](https://github.com/Jamalofski/php-src-security/commit/2c6ec3093f2d8e5cb3928f2d939ffe6a38465c18) | - | Merge branch 'PHP-5.6-security-backports' into PHP-5.6-security-backports-openssl11 | - | clean merge |  |
| 83 | [7dffbc1](https://github.com/Jamalofski/php-src-security/commit/7dffbc16e459f1c0379eb75a32bdf8a8666c4ca1) | [c14eb8d](https://github.com/php/php-src/commit/c14eb8de974fc8a4d74f3515424c293bc7a40fba) | Fix bug #78793 | - | adapted | Same lines as official c14eb8d (7.2.26), which passes data_len - offset to exif_process_IFD_TAG() for each maker-note entry. That is safe in 7.2+, where MN_OFFSET_GUESS is compiled out (official 134047d) and data_len is always value_len, but 5.6, like 7.1, still uses MN_OFFSET_GUESS for Canon maker notes, where data_len = value_len - offset_diff can be smaller than the entry offset. Defect, a regression of the backport (checked in the code at b2b7c7d): when an entry offset (2 + 12 * de) is larger than data_len, data_len - offset wraps to nearly SIZE_MAX; once the entry lies after offset_base and the value pointer after the entry, every bound check of exif_process_IFD_TAG() passes and the value is read from offset_base + offset_val (32 bits from the file) for byte_count bytes. A crafted Canon maker note thus makes exif_read_data() copy heap memory from past the maker note into its result, or crash. php-5.6.40 passed data_len, which kept the read inside the maker note. Fix: fail the entry when offset > data_len, or compile MN_OFFSET_GUESS out as 134047d does. |

| 137 | [a41cbed](https://github.com/Jamalofski/php-src-security/commit/a41cbed4532cc4d3d2fd1a8fa1a4ace5bdfcafc9) | - | Backports from 7.2.31 | - | no official commit | 5.6 port of official 1c9bd51 (bug #78875, array_len no longer cast to int) and 3c8582c (bug #78876, multipart_buffer_read() and multipart_buffer_read_body() lengths no longer int), with unsigned int where php-src uses size_t, matching 5.6's unsigned int value lengths. For multipart_buffer_read() and array_len unsigned int is enough, since those values are bounded by the multipart buffer and the header line. Defect (x64, low): multipart_buffer_read_body() keeps unsigned int total_bytes and read_bytes, so erealloc(out, total_bytes + read_bytes + 1) wraps at 4 GiB and memcpy() writes past the shrunk buffer; php-5.6.40 (int) stopped at 2 GiB with a fatal error and size_t does not wrap. It takes a single 4 GiB form field, memory_limit of 4G or more (or -1) and a body that post_max_size does not stop (post_max_size 0 or over 4G, or a chunked request). Fix: size_t, as upstream. |

| 161 | [72e14c7](https://github.com/Jamalofski/php-src-security/commit/72e14c75c5bae99a13369bf6c25c87e9c1489317) | [37962c6](https://github.com/php/php-src/commit/37962c61d29794645ec45d45d78123382d82c2e5) | Fix #80710: imap_mail_compose() header injection | - | adapted | 5.6 port of official 37962c6: header_injection() scans the NUL-terminated char * of each value (official reads ZSTR_VAL(), the same scan), and the 32 CHECK_HEADER_INJECTION() calls cover the same fields with the same labels and adrlist flags, using TSRMLS_CC and 5.6's zend_hash_find()/Z_STRVAL_PP. In the multipart-part loops the key is checked right after zend_hash_get_current_key(), but in the two loops of the top-level body the check was put before that call. Defect (checked in the code at b2b7c7d): in the type.parameters and disposition loops of the top-level body, CHECK_HEADER_INJECTION(key) runs before zend_hash_get_current_key(). key is declared without a value in imap_mail_compose() and not assigned before these loops, so the first pass reads an uninitialised pointer (crash or a spurious rejection), each later pass checks the previous key, and the last key is never checked: a CR or LF in the last parameter or disposition name of the top-level body still reaches the headers. Fix: move the check after zend_hash_get_current_key(), as in the multipart loops. Integer keys are not skipped, as in php-5.6.40 (official 8bee0fb skips them). |

| 206 | [66e67c7](https://github.com/Jamalofski/php-src-security/commit/66e67c73b83b42234530b6681dc16aac5efaf0f7) | [ac4254a](https://github.com/php/php-src/commit/ac4254ad764c70cb1f05c9270d8d12689fc3aeb6) | Increase random bytes in HTTP Digest authentication for SOAP | GHSA-76gg-c692-v2mw | adapted | Remi's 'minimal fix' for GHSA-76gg-c692-v2mw (official ac4254a, PHP 8.0.29): the cnonce is still the MD5 of php_rand() output truncated to 8 hex digits, now hashing four php_rand() values instead of one. The official fix takes 16 bytes from php_random_bytes_throw() and checks its result; that error-check half does not apply to 5.6, where php_rand() cannot fail. Remark (checked): incomplete on Windows. In thread-safe builds php_rand() is php_rand_r() from main/reentrancy.c, which keeps its state modulo PHP_RAND_MAX + 1 = 32768 (MSVC RAND_MAX), so the cnonce takes at most 32768 values; non-thread-safe builds use the CRT rand(). The predictable-cnonce part of the advisory stays open on Windows TS and partly on NTS. A complete fix would take the bytes from php_win32_get_random_bytes(). |

| 217 | [d6837c8](https://github.com/Jamalofski/php-src-security/commit/d6837c8fc4caff97716c56d6540aea0e8790a5f6) | - | NEWS | - | no official commit, NEWS/tests only |  |
| 218 | [2378783](https://github.com/Jamalofski/php-src-security/commit/237878338b1cee3119e3f6d62c640b8cdb6d1169) | [7e0e3cc](https://github.com/php/php-src/commit/7e0e3cc820c493301409a0ce2b6ef95e0ab06b0c) | Fix GHSA-w8qr-v226-r27w | GHSA-w8qr-v226-r27w | adapted | 5.6 never had the early return for bracketed IPv6 hosts that official 7e0e3cc removes: its php_filter_validate_url() only accepted alphanumerics, '-' and '.' in http(s) hosts, so '[' was refused and the is_userinfo_valid() checks always ran. To carry the 7.x change over, the backport imports the 7.0 host validation instead (a copy of _php_filter_validate_domain() with FILTER_FLAG_HOSTNAME fixed, plus php_filter_is_valid_ipv6_hostname() on the TSRMLS _php_filter_validate_ipv6() of 5.6), so FILTER_VALIDATE_URL now accepts http(s) URLs with a bracketed IPv6 host and rejects hostnames with an empty label, a '-' next to a dot, a label over 63 characters or more than 253 characters. The userinfo checks still run for every host, as in the fixed 7.x code. Remark: behaviour change against php-5.6.40, to state in PATCHES.md: FILTER_VALIDATE_URL now accepts http(s) URLs with a bracketed IPv6 host such as http://[::1]/ and rejects the hostnames listed above, which 5.6.40 accepted. |

| 243 | [31c78f1](https://github.com/Jamalofski/php-src-security/commit/31c78f16bdc84b79c7fe3d8251e20eeff66ed1ee) | [712e60f](https://github.com/php/php-src/commit/712e60f9edfd03f0960fc4fc3c2c375f42b90aa2) | Avoid state corruption via bailout | CVE-2023-0662, CVE-2026-109618, CVE-2026-109619, GHSA-6wmf-4rv5-p3rp, GHSA-w8hw-w9fx-j79w | adapted | 5.6 port of the official 7.4 end-of-life fix 712e60f through the 7.4 backport 802c210 (same changed lines as Sury's original dce84b1): memory-limit, out-of-memory and allocation-overflow errors (zend_mm_safe_error(), the six safe_address() variants, which on this branch also guard _estrdup(), _estrndup() and zend_strndup(), _safe_emalloc_string(), Balloc()) and the 'String size overflow' errors of concat_function() and add_char/string_to_string(), which back ZEND_CONCAT, ZEND_ASSIGN_CONCAT and ZEND_ADD_* in 5.6, print to stderr and _exit(1) instead of bailing out, the file and line being found the 5.6 way. The request-state resets, the multipart header filter, the size_t substring_conf() and the disabled upload progress ('|| 1') are as in 7.4, plus SG(callback_func) = NULL; GPC arrays get nTableSize 2 because CHECK_INIT() takes nTableMask 0 for an unallocated table, and since 5.6's shutdown_executor() clears the user handlers and calls zend_objects_store_destroy() on every shutdown, 7.4's fast_shutdown branch has no counterpart, the EG(error_handling)/EG(exception) reset following the block that frees the object store. max_multipart_body_parts and its limit are already on the branch (CVE-2023-0662 backport); the message calls this a hunk of the 7.4 patch, but only the official 712e60f has it. Not carried over: 7.4's exit(1) to _exit(1) in zend_out_of_memory(), whose 5.6 counterparts are __zend_malloc() and __zend_realloc() in zend_alloc.h. Remark: zend_mm_safe_error_ex() formats sizes with %zu, which the VC11 CRT prints as 'zu' (message only, the process exits anyway). With this mitigation, exhausting memory_limit ends the process (stderr, _exit(1)): with the thread-safe Apache module, the child process and its in-flight requests. |

The test expectations change accordingly and a new test covers the backslash breakout. |

The metadata error path now calls free_result(), which also frees the results of persistent connections with the right allocator instead of mnd_efree(). The binary-row bound reads row_buffer->size, which in 5.6 is the chunk size (one byte more than the data, and not updated when resize_chunk() grows a row past 16 MB), until #291 c3279aa switches it to row_buffer->app; #324 185bbdb makes the new helpers VC11-compatible. |

In 8.x MYSQLND_ROW_BUFFER.size is set to the data size, so app is the 5.6 equivalent of the official bound; with size a field could be read one byte past the received data. |

| 302 | [7a82a61](https://github.com/Jamalofski/php-src-security/commit/7a82a61409297adacc6edb91bdc73f1a491ddb11) | - | Harden mysqlnd temporal field decoding | - | no official commit | No php-src commit of its own: Ondřej Surý's 5.6 port of the mysqlnd_ps_codec.c part of the GHSA-r6x9-5r99-36j7 fix 114dbb7 (minimum lengths 8, 4 and 4 for TIME, DATE and DATETIME, second_part read only from 12 and 11 bytes, DATETIME hours from 7 bytes), identical to it apart from 5.6 types and TSRMLS, plus a check of his own that the status byte and the whole null bitmap fit the row (null_bytes is (field_count + 9) / 8 computed without overflow). The bitmap check fails before the field zvals are created, which the 5.6 callers tolerate: their fields arrays are zeroed per row and mysqlnd_rset_zval_ptr_dtor() and the buffered free_result() skip NULL entries. Incomplete (checked in the code at b2b7c7d): the NEWS entry lists GHSA-r6x9-5r99-36j7 as fixed, but the rest of 114dbb7 is not on the branch. php_mysqlnd_net_field_length() and its _ll variant read 3, 4 or 9 bytes without a bound, mysqlnd_wireprotocol.c still has only the old BAIL_IF_NO_MORE_DATA, which checks after the read (the greeting reader takes the thread id, scramble and capabilities before any check), the EOF row test is still data_size > 1 (official: >= 5), and mysqlnd_stmt_prepare_read_eof() does not get the leak fix. A malicious server can still cause small heap over-reads in the client: in 5.6 the advisory is only partly fixed. The 7.2 port 06a3234 is the closest model. |
| 303 | [f10baba](https://github.com/Jamalofski/php-src-security/commit/f10baba7086cb4e43337fdbb0bfb5c6b280dd074) | - | Reject mysqlnd OK messages past packet body | - | no official commit | No php-src counterpart: Ondřej Surý's hardening, the 5.6 form of the one reviewed on 8.1, 8.0 and 7.4 (35ba0e1, 06e44b6, 169e52d): php_mysqlnd_ok_read() bounded the OK message by the read buffer, not by the packet, so a server could have it copy bytes left in the buffer past the packet body (a NULL length marker too), and it now fails when the message goes past the body. As 5.6's php_mysqlnd_net_field_length() takes no remaining size, the size of the length prefix is checked against the rest of the body before it is decoded, so the later subtraction cannot wrap; the only side effect is that this failure path prints a wrapped byte count in its 'OK packet ... bytes shorter than expected' warning. |

| 305 | [766c0fd](https://github.com/Jamalofski/php-src-security/commit/766c0fd72ea7424fdf25d023b5c8d9d4fcd56bd3) | [7050cf3](https://github.com/php/php-src/commit/7050cf338092a93e7d4caa4b7dc0bb8771f52a01) | Fix GHSA-68fq-fh7h-f8gr: refuse keyless phar signature when .pubkey exists | GHSA-68fq-fh7h-f8gr | adapted | Same check as the official fix 7050cf3 and the 7.x backports, limited to PHAR_SIG_OPENSSL: 5.6 has no OPENSSL_SHA256/SHA512 phar signature types (added in 8.1), so phar_verify_signature() refuses any other signature type when fname.pubkey exists. Every signature path of 5.6 (phar.c, tar.c, zip.c) goes through phar_verify_signature(); has_pubkey is a zend_bool and php_stream_stat_path_ex() takes TSRMLS from the function's TSRMLS_DC. |
| 306 | [1127fb4](https://github.com/Jamalofski/php-src-security/commit/1127fb4689d38b2a5bac06a4d352a1a9f8f88da9) | [964cdcf](https://github.com/php/php-src/commit/964cdcfa5adfa577cde97df89fc8fda04815694d) | Fix GHSA-5j3f-64r7-f564: PATH_INFO underflow in CGI init_request_info() | CVE-2019-11043, GHSA-5j3f-64r7-f564 | adapted | Same added and removed lines as official 964cdcf in init_request_info() of the CGI SAPI (PATH_INFO rewritten only when it is longer than the suffix stripped from SCRIPT_FILENAME); only the context differs: TSRMLS_DC, and slen and pilen are int in 5.6. Both are non-negative there (slen counts the characters stripped from a copy of SCRIPT_FILENAME), so the signed pilen > slen test is the official guard, and path_info is not used outside the guarded block. |
| 307 | [1b3cbf1](https://github.com/Jamalofski/php-src-security/commit/1b3cbf165881bd1d71ddb798ad352d45e315f32a) | [1ecb92f](https://github.com/php/php-src/commit/1ecb92f2cdeb235bb10d6c220c9be4a13f527243) | Fix GHSA-3mg3-cr28-46gc: FPM XSS within status endpoint via user and script | GHSA-3mg3-cr28-46gc, GHSA-7qg2-v9fj-4mwv | adapted | The 7.2 backport b2fdf24 (reviewed) on 5.6 code: the same fpm_status_escape() helper for the request method, URI, query string, user and script fields of the full status, with 5.6's char * php_escape_html_entities_ex() (length out parameter, TSRMLS_CC) and an efree() of each escaped copy, which also fixes the old leak of an empty escaped string. As on 7.x, only the html and xml outputs set encode; the JSON output escapes none of its strings, as before (JSON escaping came with 8.1). |
| 308 | [2666934](https://github.com/Jamalofski/php-src-security/commit/2666934c850de082a89f311ce6c3940b63a4c958) | [28014fd](https://github.com/php/php-src/commit/28014fd092c7acdafb9c074e735f099893a5ef66) | Fix GHSA-qc49-hpvq-g2w6: Drain unread request bodies | GHSA-qc49-hpvq-g2w6 | adapted | Same changes as official 28014fd: sapi_deactivate() drains unread request input even when php://input created a request_body stream, and fcgi_read_request() refuses a new request while a STDIN record is only partly read, with in_len reset in fcgi_close(). 5.6 has no main/fastcgi.c, so the FastCGI change is applied identically to its two copies, sapi/cgi/fastcgi.c (php-cgi, the FastCGI SAPI of the Windows builds) and sapi/fpm/fpm/fastcgi.c; a refused request ends in fcgi_close(req, 1, 1) as in 8.x. |
| 309 | [b3c199e](https://github.com/Jamalofski/php-src-security/commit/b3c199eef48133ccfea27e810394e85bf3e65cc6) | [66db5cd](https://github.com/php/php-src/commit/66db5cda7df3d7a0e1fc7b171bd9ca318fca49fb) | Fix GHSA-4646-p932-gw34: keep SOAP DTD alive during cleanup | GHSA-4646-p932-gw34 | identical |  |
| 310 | [6a66475](https://github.com/Jamalofski/php-src-security/commit/6a664759dd5c3754486d7057b81195ca0acb489b) | [f986c5c](https://github.com/php/php-src/commit/f986c5ca37c0fdc50595d8291a9920a1df23bf38) | Fix GHSA-gr4f-wgx5-jpfv: DATEG(tzcache) use-after-free | GHSA-gr4f-wgx5-jpfv | adapted | Same change to php_log_err() as official f986c5c: the log time uses the local time zone only when PG(modules_activated) && EG(active), so no DATEG(tzcache) is created during module startup (whose heap shutdown_memory_manager() frees, leaving the pointer dangling) or used during request startup before the date RINIT; it replaces 5.6's ZTS-only php_during_module_startup() test. 5.6 has the same request startup order as 8.x (php_hash_environment() before zend_activate_modules()) and both globals; the official zend_test INI entry is not ported because 5.6 has no zend_test, and its test came separately (77f1823). |

Only the context differs (TSRMLS_CC, '#if defined(HAVE_PWRITE)'); ftruncate() was already used there on Windows. |

An entry whose offset is past data_len is refused with a warning, before data_len - offset can wrap; php-5.6.40 never reached that state. bug78793_guess.phpt builds such a maker note: with the fix the entries past the data are absent, without it entry 9 is read from past the 122-byte maker note. |
| 331 | [c211937](https://github.com/Jamalofski/php-src-security/commit/c211937d3357496c161f408114757f361610fc2b) | - | Fix #80710 for 5.6: check the parameter names of the top-level body | - | no official commit | Ours, to propose upstream: in the type.parameters and disposition loops of the top-level body of imap_mail_compose(), the key is now fetched before CHECK_HEADER_INJECTION(key) (it was checked first, uninitialised on the first pass, and the last name never), and before the parameter is allocated, so that a rejected name leaks nothing. The multipart loops already fetched it first. bug80710_3.phpt puts the injected name last in both loops. |
| 332 | [385cebd](https://github.com/Jamalofski/php-src-security/commit/385cebd266e30742aee404c211d65c388dd91019) | [e840071](https://github.com/php/php-src/commit/e840071b7653a205d4d00c769cc48f180a5c16b0) | Never return private part of the key from openssl_csr_get_public_key | - | adapted | Port of php-src e840071 (part of the 7.0 port to OpenSSL 1.1, missing from 1633657): with OpenSSL 1.1 and later, the request is duplicated with X509_REQ_dup() before X509_REQ_get_pubkey() and freed after, so that openssl_csr_get_public_key() no longer returns the private key given to openssl_csr_new() in the same script. Same lines as the official commit, plus the NULL check of the key that 7.0 already had (5.6 registered a NULL key). openssl_csr_get_public_key_private.phpt checks that the key has no 'd'. |
| 333 | [888ebe0](https://github.com/Jamalofski/php-src-security/commit/888ebe0736853de400aaf1d24c2fce1229d1ed3d) | - | Fix use after free of the openssl_sign() result in phar | - | no official commit | Ours, to propose upstream: phar_call_openssl_signverify(), the path of builds without native OpenSSL in phar (Windows), now reads Z_BVAL_P(retval_ptr) before efree(retval_ptr), as its IS_LONG branch does; the bug is in php-5.6.40 (7.0 keeps the return value on the stack). Only openssl_sign() returns a bool, so signing failed at random and verification was not affected. phar_setsignaturealgo_openssl_repeat.phpt signs 100 times. |
| 334 | [2530d40](https://github.com/Jamalofski/php-src-security/commit/2530d40ce9cbe12a22a83f3e2dc79c4f4ce0ab1e) | - | Fix bug #78876 for 5.6: bound the length of a POST variable | - | no official commit | Ours, to propose upstream: multipart_buffer_read_body() no longer grows a value past INT_MAX - 1 bytes, the largest PHP 5 string, so that total_bytes + read_bytes + 1 cannot wrap on 64-bit builds (a41cbed made the lengths unsigned int); the value is dropped with a warning and the rest of the part is still read, so that the next parts are parsed. No test: it takes a 2 GiB field. |
| 335 | [d1b24d6](https://github.com/Jamalofski/php-src-security/commit/d1b24d6c175d7ca28fbbbc150e315b15f83f4349) | [114dbb7](https://github.com/php/php-src/commit/114dbb74368e6a6cd6e48ddb4a76dbe8728b84e2) | Fix GHSA-r6x9-5r99-36j7: Various packet overreads in mysqlnd wireprotocol | GHSA-r6x9-5r99-36j7 | adapted | Ours, to propose upstream: 5.6 port of 114dbb7 after the 7.3 port 8ea25e2 (reviewed) and 06a3234. Same added checks, in 5.6 code: bounded php_mysqlnd_net_field_length() and _ll() (MYSQLND_INVALID_NET_FIELD_LENGTH, MYSQLND_OPTIONAL_UINT64_T) and all their callers in the readers and ps_codec, BAIL_IF_NOT_ENOUGH_DATA_EX() before the reads of the greeting (with the rest of the scramble, which 06a3234 left out), auth, OK, EOF, result set header, field (13 fixed bytes), prepare, change user and sha256 readers, NUL-terminated server version and plugin names, an EOF row of 5 bytes, and the release of what the statement owns in the error path of mysqlnd_stmt_prepare_read_eof() (also the connection reference of the result, as in 8ea25e2). Kept from 5.6: Sury's stricter OK message check (7a82a61 and f10baba) and the default value of COM_FIELD_LIST fields, which 5.6 still reads for mysql_list_fields(), now with the bounded decoder. NEWS lists the advisory as fixed. The 34 tests that apply to 5.6 (all but caching_sha2_password) and their fake server scenarios, in PHP 5 syntax, with SKIPIF. |
| 336 | [883374c](https://github.com/Jamalofski/php-src-security/commit/883374cba88523021e04324b5d90cc61cb49de17) | - | Fix GHSA-r6x9-5r99-36j7 tests for 5.6 | GHSA-r6x9-5r99-36j7 | no official commit, NEWS/tests only |  |
| 337 | [61cd630](https://github.com/Jamalofski/php-src-security/commit/61cd630a9156112cba47ec8dbb6a63bf671b9856) | - | Fix GHSA-r6x9-5r99-36j7 for 5.6: field packet checks in the official order | GHSA-r6x9-5r99-36j7 | no official commit | Ours, to propose upstream: follow-up of d1b24d6. In php_mysqlnd_rset_field_read() the length byte of the fixed block is read after a one-byte check and its 'Expected 12' warning comes before the check of the 13 bytes of the block, as in 114dbb7 (d1b24d6 checked the 13 bytes first, as 06a3234). The tests get the output of 5.6: the COM_QUIT that the fake server reports after 'done!', 'INIT_DB's OK packet' and 'Error while reading a row' in 5.6 wording, no generic warning where Sury's OK message check (f10baba) bails first, and a skip of the sha256 tests when mysqlnd has no sha256_password plugin. |

## Commits to review

Adapted commits: differences between the official patch and the backport in files that go into the binaries (hunk line numbers ignored). Commits without an official counterpart: their whole patch.

<details><summary>#1 1633657 backport needed changes for OpenSSL 1.1</summary>

```diff
+++ backport/ext/openssl/openssl.c
--- a/ext/openssl/openssl.c
+++ b/ext/openssl/openssl.c
@@

 #define OPENSSL_ALGO_MD2	4

+#if OPENSSL_VERSION_NUMBER < 0x10100000L || defined (LIBRESSL_VERSION_NUMBER)

+#endif
 #if OPENSSL_VERSION_NUMBER >= 0x0090708fL
 #define OPENSSL_ALGO_SHA224 6
 #define OPENSSL_ALGO_SHA256 7
@@ static int add_oid_section(struct php_x509_request * req TSRMLS_DC) /* {{{ */
 	}
 	for (i = 0; i < sk_CONF_VALUE_num(sktmp); i++) {
 		cnf = sk_CONF_VALUE_value(sktmp, i);
-		if (OBJ_create(cnf->value, cnf->name, cnf->name) == NID_undef) {
+		if (OBJ_sn2nid(cnf->name) == NID_undef && OBJ_ln2nid(cnf->name) == NID_undef &&
+				OBJ_create(cnf->value, cnf->name, cnf->name) == NID_undef) {
 			php_error_docref(NULL TSRMLS_CC, E_WARNING, "problem creating object %s=%s", cnf->name, cnf->value);
 			return FAILURE;
 		}
@@ static EVP_MD * php_openssl_get_evp_md_from_algo(long algo) { /* {{{ */
 			mdtype = (EVP_MD *) EVP_md2();
 			break;
 #endif
+#if OPENSSL_VERSION_NUMBER < 0x10100000L || defined (LIBRESSL_VERSION_NUMBER)
 		case OPENSSL_ALGO_DSS1:
 			mdtype = (EVP_MD *) EVP_dss1();
 			break;
+#endif
 #if OPENSSL_VERSION_NUMBER >= 0x0090708fL
 		case OPENSSL_ALGO_SHA224:
 			mdtype = (EVP_MD *) EVP_sha224();
@@ PHP_MINIT_FUNCTION(openssl)
 #ifdef HAVE_OPENSSL_MD2_H
 	REGISTER_LONG_CONSTANT("OPENSSL_ALGO_MD2", OPENSSL_ALGO_MD2, CONST_CS|CONST_PERSISTENT);
 #endif
+#if OPENSSL_VERSION_NUMBER < 0x10100000L || defined (LIBRESSL_VERSION_NUMBER)
 	REGISTER_LONG_CONSTANT("OPENSSL_ALGO_DSS1", OPENSSL_ALGO_DSS1, CONST_CS|CONST_PERSISTENT);
+#endif
 #if OPENSSL_VERSION_NUMBER >= 0x0090708fL

 	REGISTER_LONG_CONSTANT("OPENSSL_ALGO_SHA256", OPENSSL_ALGO_SHA256, CONST_CS|CONST_PERSISTENT);

 {
 	GENERAL_NAMES *names;

+	ASN1_OCTET_STRING *extension_data;
 	long i, length, num;
 	const unsigned char *p;
 
@@ static int openssl_x509v3_subjectAltName(BIO *bio, X509_EXTENSION *extension)
 		return -1;
 	}
 
-	p = extension->value->data;
-	length = extension->value->length;
+	extension_data = X509_EXTENSION_get_data(extension);
+	p = extension_data->data;
+	length = extension_data->length;

 		names = (GENERAL_NAMES*)(ASN1_item_d2i(NULL, &p, length,
 						       ASN1_ITEM_ptr(method->it)));
@@ PHP_FUNCTION(openssl_x509_parse)
 	char * tmpstr;
 	zval * subitem;
 	X509_EXTENSION *extension;
+	X509_NAME *subject_name;
+	char *cert_name;
 	char *extname;

 	BUF_MEM *bio_buf;
@@ PHP_FUNCTION(openssl_x509_parse)
 	}
 	array_init(return_value);
 
-	if (cert->name) {
-		add_assoc_string(return_value, "name", cert->name, 1);
-	}
+	subject_name = X509_get_subject_name(cert);
+	cert_name = X509_NAME_oneline(subject_name, NULL, 0);
+	add_assoc_string(return_value, "name", cert_name, 1);
+	OPENSSL_free(cert_name);

 /*	add_assoc_bool(return_value, "valid", cert->valid); */

+/* {{{ php_openssl_dh_pub_from_priv */
+static BIGNUM *php_openssl_dh_pub_from_priv(BIGNUM *priv_key, BIGNUM *g, BIGNUM *p)
+{
+	BIGNUM *pub_key, *priv_key_const_time;
+	BN_CTX *ctx;
+
+	pub_key = BN_new();
+	if (pub_key == NULL) {
+		return NULL;
+	}
+
+	priv_key_const_time = BN_new();
+	if (priv_key_const_time == NULL) {
+		BN_free(pub_key);
+		return NULL;
+	}
+	ctx = BN_CTX_new();
+	if (ctx == NULL) {
+		BN_free(pub_key);
+		BN_free(priv_key_const_time);
+		return NULL;
+	}
+
+	BN_with_flags(priv_key_const_time, priv_key, BN_FLG_CONSTTIME);
+
+	if (!BN_mod_exp_mont(pub_key, g, priv_key_const_time, p, ctx, NULL)) {
+		BN_free(pub_key);
+		pub_key = NULL;
+	}
+
+	BN_free(priv_key_const_time);
+	BN_CTX_free(ctx);
+
+	return pub_key;
+}
+/* }}} */
```

<details><summary>#3 a7b15f6 Backport SETUP_OPENSSL() and the ext/curl config</summary>

```diff
+++ backport/ext/curl/config.w32
--- a/ext/curl/config.w32
+++ b/ext/curl/config.w32
@@
 ARG_WITH("curl", "cURL support", "no");
 
 if (PHP_CURL != "no") {
+	var ver_num = NaN;
+	var f = PHP_PHP_BUILD + "/include/curl/curlver.h";
+	if (FSO.FileExists(f)) {
+		var reg = /LIBCURL_VERSION_NUM\s+(0x[a-z0-9]+)/gi;
+		var m = reg.exec(file_get_contents(PHP_PHP_BUILD + "/include/curl/curlver.h"));
+		if (!!m && m.length >= 2) {
+			ver_num = parseInt(m[1]);
+		}
+	}
+
 	if (CHECK_LIB("libcurl_a.lib;libcurl.lib", "curl", PHP_CURL) &&
-			CHECK_HEADER_ADD_INCLUDE("curl/easy.h", "CFLAGS_CURL") &&
-			CHECK_LIB("ssleay32.lib", "curl", PHP_CURL) &&
-			CHECK_LIB("libeay32.lib", "curl", PHP_CURL) 
-		&& CHECK_LIB("winmm.lib", "curl", PHP_CURL)
-		&& CHECK_LIB("wldap32.lib", "curl", PHP_CURL)
-		&& (((PHP_ZLIB=="no") && (CHECK_LIB("zlib_a.lib;zlib.lib", "curl", PHP_CURL))) || 
-			(PHP_ZLIB_SHARED && CHECK_LIB("zlib.lib", "curl", PHP_CURL)) || (PHP_ZLIB == "yes" && (!PHP_ZLIB_SHARED)))
+		CHECK_HEADER_ADD_INCLUDE("curl/easy.h", "CFLAGS_CURL") &&
+		SETUP_OPENSSL("curl", PHP_CURL) > 0 &&
+		CHECK_LIB("winmm.lib", "curl", PHP_CURL) &&
+		CHECK_LIB("wldap32.lib", "curl", PHP_CURL) &&
+		(((PHP_ZLIB=="no") && (CHECK_LIB("zlib_a.lib;zlib.lib", "curl", PHP_CURL))) ||
+			(PHP_ZLIB_SHARED && CHECK_LIB("zlib.lib", "curl", PHP_CURL)) || (PHP_ZLIB == "yes" && (!PHP_ZLIB_SHARED))) &&
+		!isNaN(ver_num) &&
+		(ver_num <= parseInt("0x073b00") || ver_num > parseInt("0x073b00") &&
+			CHECK_LIB("normaliz.lib", "curl", PHP_CURL) &&
+			CHECK_LIB("libssh2.lib", "curl", PHP_CURL))
 		) {
-		EXTENSION("curl", "interface.c multi.c share.c curl_file.c", true);
+		EXTENSION("curl", "interface.c multi.c share.c curl_file.c");
 		AC_DEFINE('HAVE_CURL', 1, 'Have cURL library');
 		AC_DEFINE('HAVE_CURL_SSL', 1, 'Have SSL suppurt in cURL');
 		AC_DEFINE('HAVE_CURL_EASY_STRERROR', 1, 'Have curl_easy_strerror in cURL');
+++ backport/win32/build/confutils.js
--- a/win32/build/confutils.js
+++ b/win32/build/confutils.js
@@ if (!MODE_PHPIZE) {
 	ARG_ENABLE('one-shot', 'Optimize for fast build - best for release and snapshot builders, not so hot for edit-and-rebuild hacking', 'no');

 }

 

+function SETUP_OPENSSL(target, path_to_check, common_name, use_env, add_dir_part, add_to_flag_only)

+{

+	var ret = 0;

+	var cflags_var = "CFLAGS_" + target.toUpperCase();

+

+	if (CHECK_LIB("libcrypto.lib", target, path_to_check) &&

+			CHECK_LIB("libssl.lib", target, path_to_check) &&

+			CHECK_LIB("crypt32.lib", target, path_to_check, common_name) &&

+			CHECK_HEADER_ADD_INCLUDE("openssl/ssl.h", cflags_var, path_to_check, use_env, add_dir_part, add_to_flag_only)) {

+		/* Openssl 1.1.x */

+		return 2;

+	} else if (CHECK_LIB("ssleay32.lib", target, path_to_check, common_name) &&

+			CHECK_LIB("libeay32.lib", target, path_to_check, common_name) &&

+			CHECK_LIB("crypt32.lib", target, path_to_check, common_name) &&

+			CHECK_HEADER_ADD_INCLUDE("openssl/ssl.h", cflags_var, path_to_check, use_env, add_dir_part, add_to_flag_only)) {

+		/* Openssl 1.0.x and lower */

+		return 1;

+	}

+

+	return ret;

+}

+
```

</details>

```diff
 @@ struct sqlite3_api_routines {

+                          void(*)(void*), unsigned char);
+   int (*strglob)(const char*,const char*);

++  sqlite3_value *(*value_dup)(const sqlite3_value*);

++  int (*result_zeroblob64)(sqlite3_context*,sqlite3_uint64);

++  void (*result_subtype)(sqlite3_context*,unsigned int);
++  /* Version 3.10.0 and later */
++  int (*status64)(int,sqlite3_int64*,sqlite3_int64*,int);
++  int (*strlike)(const char*,const char*,unsigned int);
++  int (*db_cacheflush)(sqlite3*);
++  /* Version 3.12.0 and later */
++  int (*system_errno)(sqlite3*);
++  /* Version 3.14.0 and later */
++  int (*trace_v2)(sqlite3*,unsigned,int(*)(unsigned,void*,void*,void*),void*);
++  char *(*expanded_sql)(sqlite3_stmt*);
 +  /* Version 3.18.0 and later */
@@ -66,4 +98,25 @@
  
++/*
++** This is the function signature used for all extension entry points.  It
++** is also defined in the file "loadext.c".
++*/
++typedef int (*sqlite3_loadext_entry)(

++  char **pzErrMsg,                   /* Used to set error string on failure. */
++  const sqlite3_api_routines *pThunk /* Extension API function pointers. */
++);
++
  /*
-@@ typedef int (*sqlite3_loadext_entry)(

- /* Version 3.14.0 and later */
```

```diff
++
++	/* Ignore trailing dot */

++		e = t;
++		l--;
++	}

++	/* The total length cannot exceed 253 characters (final dot not included) */
++	if (l > 253) {
++		return 0;
++	}
++

++	if(*s == '.' || (hostname && !isalnum((int)*(unsigned char *)s))) {
++		return 0;
++	}

++		if (*s == '.') {
++			/* The first and the last character of a label must be alphanumeric */
++			if (*(s + 1) == '.' || (hostname && (!isalnum((int)*(unsigned char *)(s - 1)) || !isalnum((int)*(unsigned char *)(s + 1))))) {
++				return 0;
++			}
++
++			/* Reset label length counter */
++			i = 1;
++		} else {
++			if (i > 63 || (hostname && *s != '-' && !isalnum((int)*(unsigned char *)s))) {
++				return 0;
++			}
++
++			i++;
++		}
++
++		s++;
++	}
++
++	return 1;
```

<details><summary>#242 5c2cc46 ext/openssl: openssl_encrypt() zend mm heap overflow on AES-WRAP-PAD mode.</summary>

```diff
# different changes in ext/openssl/openssl.c
--- official/ext/openssl/openssl.c
+++ backport/ext/openssl/openssl.c
@@ -2,15 +2,6 @@
 +++ b/ext/openssl/openssl.c
-@@ static int php_openssl_cipher_update(const EVP_CIPHER *cipher_type,
- 		const char *aad, size_t aad_len, int enc)  /* {{{ */
- {
- 	int i = 0;
-+	size_t outlen = data_len + EVP_CIPHER_block_size(cipher_type);
+@@ PHP_FUNCTION(openssl_encrypt)
+ 	free_iv = php_openssl_validate_iv(&iv, &iv_len, max_iv_len TSRMLS_CC);
  
- 	if (mode->is_single_run_aead && !EVP_CipherUpdate(cipher_ctx, NULL, &i, NULL, (int)data_len)) {
- 		php_openssl_store_errors();
-@@ static int php_openssl_cipher_update(const EVP_CIPHER *cipher_type,
- 		return FAILURE;
- 	}
- 
--	*poutbuf = zend_string_alloc((int)data_len + EVP_CIPHER_block_size(cipher_type), 0);
+ 	outlen = data_len + EVP_CIPHER_block_size(cipher_type);
 +#ifdef EVP_CIPH_WRAP_MODE
@@ -26,15 +17,22 @@
 +#endif
-+
-+	*poutbuf = zend_string_alloc(outlen, false);
+ 	outbuf = safe_emalloc(outlen, 1, 1);
  
- 	if (!EVP_CipherUpdate(cipher_ctx, (unsigned char*)ZSTR_VAL(*poutbuf),
- 					&i, (const unsigned char *)data, (int)data_len)) {
-@@ static int php_openssl_cipher_update(const EVP_CIPHER *cipher_type,
- 		}
- 		*/
- 		php_openssl_store_errors();
--		zend_string_release_ex(*poutbuf, 0);
-+		zend_string_release_ex(*poutbuf, false);
- 		return FAILURE;
- 	}
+ 	EVP_EncryptInit(cipher_ctx, cipher_type, NULL, NULL);
+@@ PHP_FUNCTION(openssl_decrypt)
+ 	free_iv = php_openssl_validate_iv(&iv, &iv_len, EVP_CIPHER_iv_length(cipher_type) TSRMLS_CC);
  
+ 	outlen = data_len + EVP_CIPHER_block_size(cipher_type);
++#ifdef EVP_CIPH_WRAP_MODE
++	if ((EVP_CIPHER_mode(cipher_type)) == EVP_CIPH_WRAP_MODE) {
++		/*
++		 * RFC 5649 wrap-with-padding rounds the input up to the block size
++		 * and prepends an integrity block, we reserve one extra block.
++		 * See EVP_EncryptUpdate(3): wrap mode may write up to
++		 * inl + cipher_block_size bytes.
++		 */
++		outlen += EVP_CIPHER_block_size(cipher_type);
++	}
++#endif
+ 	outbuf = emalloc(outlen + 1);
+ 
+ 	EVP_DecryptInit(cipher_ctx, cipher_type, NULL, NULL);
```

<details><summary>#243 31c78f1 Avoid state corruption via bailout</summary>

```diff
# 1 file(s) with the same added and removed lines (context differs); different changes in Zend/zend_API.h, Zend/zend_alloc.c, Zend/zend_alloc.h, Zend/zend_execute_API.c, Zend/zend_strtod.c, ext/session/session.c, main/SAPI.c, main/main.c, main/php_variables.c, main/rfc1867.c; official changes not applied to Zend/zend_hash.c, Zend/zend_hash.h, Zend/zend_multiply.h, Zend/zend_types.h, Zend/zend_vm_def.h, Zend/zend_vm_execute.h; backport-only changes to Zend/zend_API.c, Zend/zend_operators.c

++}
++/* }}} */

+ static int zend_merge_property(zval **value TSRMLS_DC, int num_args, va_list args, const zend_hash_key *hash_key) /* {{{ */
```

<details><summary>#244 bd94d69 Fix SQL injection in ext-pgsql via E'...' backslash breakout</summary>

```diff
# different changes in ext/pgsql/pgsql.c
--- official/ext/pgsql/pgsql.c
+++ backport/ext/pgsql/pgsql.c
@@ -2,18 +2,17 @@
 +++ b/ext/pgsql/pgsql.c
-@@ static int php_pgsql_convert_match(const zend_string *str, const char *regex , s
-  */
- static zend_string *php_pgsql_add_quotes(zend_string *src)
- {
--	return zend_string_concat3("E'", strlen("E'"), ZSTR_VAL(src), ZSTR_LEN(src), "'", strlen("'"));
-+	return zend_string_concat3("'", strlen("'"), ZSTR_VAL(src), ZSTR_LEN(src), "'", strlen("'"));
- }
- /* }}} */
+@@ static int php_pgsql_add_quotes(zval *src, zend_bool should_free TSRMLS_DC)
+ 	assert(Z_TYPE_P(src) == IS_STRING);
+ 	assert(should_free == 1 || should_free == 0);
  
-@@ PHP_PGSQL_API zend_result php_pgsql_convert(PGconn *pg_link, const zend_string *
- 							zend_string *str;
+-	smart_str_appendc(&str, 'E');
+ 	smart_str_appendc(&str, '\'');
+ 	smart_str_appendl(&str, Z_STRVAL_P(src), Z_STRLEN_P(src));
+ 	smart_str_appendc(&str, '\'');
+@@ PHP_PGSQL_API int php_pgsql_convert(PGconn *pg_link, const char *table_name, con
  							/* PostgreSQL ignores \0 */
- 							str = zend_string_alloc(Z_STRLEN_P(val) * 2, 0);
+ 							Z_TYPE_P(new_val) = IS_STRING;
+ 							tmp = (char *)safe_emalloc(Z_STRLEN_PP(val), 2, 1);
 -							/* better to use PGSQLescapeLiteral since PGescapeStringConn does not handle special \ */
- 							ZSTR_LEN(str) = PQescapeStringConn(pg_link, ZSTR_VAL(str),
- 									Z_STRVAL_P(val), Z_STRLEN_P(val), &escape_err);
- 							if (escape_err) {
+ 							Z_STRLEN_P(new_val) = (int)PQescapeStringConn(pg_link, tmp, Z_STRVAL_PP(val), Z_STRLEN_PP(val), NULL);
+ 							Z_STRVAL_P(new_val) = tmp;
+ 							php_pgsql_add_quotes(new_val, 1 TSRMLS_CC);
```

</details>

```diff
+
```

```diff
  	/* count the number of ' characters */
```

<details><summary>#272 5aa8592 GHSA-85c2-q967-79q5: [soap] Fix stale SOAP_GLOBAL(ref_map) pointer with Apache Map</summary>

```diff
# different changes in ext/soap/php_encoding.c
--- official/ext/soap/php_encoding.c
+++ backport/ext/soap/php_encoding.c
@@ -2,18 +2,13 @@
 +++ b/ext/soap/php_encoding.c
-@@ static bool soap_check_xml_ref(zval *data, xmlNodePtr node)
- static void soap_add_xml_ref(zval *data, xmlNodePtr node)
- {
- 	if (SOAP_GLOBAL(ref_map)) {
-+		Z_TRY_ADDREF_P(data);
- 		zend_hash_index_update(SOAP_GLOBAL(ref_map), (zend_ulong)node, data);
+@@ static zend_bool soap_check_xml_ref(zval **data, xmlNodePtr node TSRMLS_DC)
+ 				return 1;
+ 			}
+ 		} else {
++			/* Hold a reference, the value may be released (e.g. a duplicate Apache Map key)
++			   while it is still reachable through href. Only decoding stores zvals in the
++			   map, encoding stores xmlNodePtr, so the destructor is set here. */
++			Z_ADDREF_PP(data);
++			SOAP_GLOBAL(ref_map)->pDestructor = ZVAL_PTR_DTOR;
+ 			zend_hash_index_update(SOAP_GLOBAL(ref_map), (ulong)node, (void**)data, sizeof(zval*), NULL);
+ 		}
  	}
- }
-@@ void encode_reset_ns()
- 	} else {
- 		SOAP_GLOBAL(ref_map) = emalloc(sizeof(HashTable));
- 	}
--	zend_hash_init(SOAP_GLOBAL(ref_map), 0, NULL, NULL, 0);
-+	zend_hash_init(SOAP_GLOBAL(ref_map), 0, NULL, ZVAL_PTR_DTOR, 0);
- }
- 
- void encode_finish()
```

<details><summary>#296 e8c4d3e fix GHSA-85c2-q967-79q5 for 5.6: do not free the returned value while ref_map still holds it</summary>

```diff
+++ backport/ext/soap/php_packet_soap.c
--- a/ext/soap/php_packet_soap.c
+++ b/ext/soap/php_packet_soap.c
@@ int parse_packet_soap(zval *this_ptr, char *buffer, int buffer_size, sdlFunction
 			tmp = *(zval**)tmp;
 			Z_ADDREF_P(tmp);
 			zval_dtor(return_value);
-			*return_value = *tmp;
-			FREE_ZVAL(tmp);
+			/* tmp may still be referenced from SOAP_GLOBAL(ref_map), so it must not be freed here */
+			ZVAL_COPY_VALUE(return_value, tmp);
+			zval_copy_ctor(return_value);
+			zval_ptr_dtor(&tmp);
 		}
 	}
 
```

<details><summary>#302 7a82a61 Harden mysqlnd temporal field decoding</summary>

```diff
+++ backport/NEWS
--- a/NEWS
+++ b/NEWS
@@ Backported from 8.2.34
   . Fixed GHSA-62xp-839h-2637 (IPv6 ACL bypass in FastCGI listen.allowed_clients
     due to partial address comparison). (CVE-2026-91768) (Alexandre Daubois)
 
+- MySQLnd:
+  . Hardened binary temporal field decoding against short lengths
+    (GHSA-r6x9-5r99-36j7, CVE-2025-1218).
+
 - OpenSSL:
   . Fixed GHSA-vvx9-73fr-5jjx (TLS hostname verification falls back to CN after
     SAN mismatch). (CVE-2026-91769) (Jakub Zelenka)
+++ backport/ext/mysqli/tests/fake_server.inc
--- a/ext/mysqli/tests/fake_server.inc
+++ b/ext/mysqli/tests/fake_server.inc
@@ function my_mysqli_test_stmt_response_row_over_read_string(my_mysqli_fake_server
     $conn->read(65536);
 }
 
+function my_mysqli_test_stmt_response_row_short_length(my_mysqli_fake_server_conn $conn, $field_name)
+{
+    $rh = $conn->packet_generator->server_stmt_execute_data_response($field_name);
+    /* The declared length cannot cover the fixed offsets of this field type. */
+    $rh[4]->packet_length = '090000';
+    $rh[4]->row_field2 = '01de';
+
+    $conn->send_server_greetings();
+    $conn->read();
+    $conn->send_server_ok();
+    $conn->read();
+    $conn->send_server_stmt_prepare_data_response($field_name);
+    $conn->read();
+    $conn->send($conn->packets_to_bytes($rh),
+        "Malicious Stmt Response for data $field_name [length too short]");
+    $conn->read(65536);
+}
```

```diff
+    $p->message_len = '10';

+    $conn->send_server_greetings();
+    $conn->read();
+    $conn->send_server_ok();
+    $conn->read();
+    $conn->send($p->to_bytes(), 'OK message length past packet');
+    $conn->read();
+}
```

<details><summary>#308 2666934 Fix GHSA-qc49-hpvq-g2w6: Drain unread request bodies</summary>

```diff
# 1 file(s) with the same added and removed lines (context differs); official changes not applied to main/fastcgi.c; backport-only changes to sapi/cgi/fastcgi.c, sapi/fpm/fpm/fastcgi.c
--- official/main/SAPI.c
+++ backport/main/SAPI.c
@@ -2,3 +2,3 @@
 +++ b/main/SAPI.c
-@@ SAPI_API void sapi_deactivate_module(void)
+@@ SAPI_API void sapi_deactivate(TSRMLS_D)
  	zend_llist_destroy(&SG(sapi_headers).headers);
--- official/main/fastcgi.c
+++ backport/main/fastcgi.c
@@ -1,22 +0,0 @@
---- a/main/fastcgi.c
-+++ b/main/fastcgi.c
-@@ static int fcgi_read_request(fcgi_request *req)
- 	int len, padding;
- 	unsigned char buf[FCGI_MAX_LENGTH+8];
- 
-+	if (UNEXPECTED(req->in_len != 0)) {
-+		return 0;
-+	}
-+
- 	req->keep = 0;
- 	req->ended = 0;
- 	req->in_len = 0;
-@@ void fcgi_close(fcgi_request *req, int force, int destroy)
- 		req->nodelay = 0;
- #endif
- 		req->fd = -1;
-+		/* a partially read STDIN record does not outlive its connection */
-+		req->in_len = 0;
- 
- 		req->hook.on_close();
- 	}
--- official/sapi/cgi/fastcgi.c
+++ backport/sapi/cgi/fastcgi.c
@@ -0,0 +1,22 @@
+--- a/sapi/cgi/fastcgi.c
++++ b/sapi/cgi/fastcgi.c
+@@ static int fcgi_read_request(fcgi_request *req)
+ 	int len, padding;
+ 	unsigned char buf[FCGI_MAX_LENGTH+8];
+ 
++	if (UNEXPECTED(req->in_len != 0)) {
++		return 0;
++	}
++
+ 	req->keep = 0;
+ 	req->closed = 0;
+ 	req->in_len = 0;
+@@ static inline void fcgi_close(fcgi_request *req, int force, int destroy)
+ 		req->nodelay = 0;
+ #endif
+ 		req->fd = -1;
++		/* a partially read STDIN record does not outlive its connection */
++		req->in_len = 0;
+ 	}
+ }
+ 
--- official/sapi/fpm/fpm/fastcgi.c
+++ backport/sapi/fpm/fpm/fastcgi.c
@@ -0,0 +1,22 @@
+--- a/sapi/fpm/fpm/fastcgi.c
++++ b/sapi/fpm/fpm/fastcgi.c
+@@ static int fcgi_read_request(fcgi_request *req)
+ 	int len, padding;
+ 	unsigned char buf[FCGI_MAX_LENGTH+8];
+ 
++	if (UNEXPECTED(req->in_len != 0)) {
++		return 0;
++	}
++
+ 	req->keep = 0;
+ 	req->closed = 0;
+ 	req->in_len = 0;
+@@ void fcgi_close(fcgi_request *req, int force, int destroy)
+ 		close(req->fd);
+ #endif
+ 		req->fd = -1;
++		/* a partially read STDIN record does not outlive its connection */
++		req->in_len = 0;
+ 		fpm_request_finished();
+ 	}
+ }
```

<details><summary>#325 a6230e6 Harden mysqlnd temporal field decoding: VC11 compatibility</summary>

```diff
+++ backport/ext/mysqlnd/mysqlnd_wireprotocol.c
--- a/ext/mysqlnd/mysqlnd_wireprotocol.c
+++ b/ext/mysqlnd/mysqlnd_wireprotocol.c
@@ php_mysqlnd_rowp_read_binary_protocol(MYSQLND_MEMORY_POOL_CHUNK * row_buffer, zv
 	zend_uchar * p = rbp;
 	zend_uchar * null_ptr, bit;
 	zval **current_field, **end_field, **start_field;
+	size_t null_bytes;
 
 	DBG_ENTER("php_mysqlnd_rowp_read_binary_protocol");
 
@@ php_mysqlnd_rowp_read_binary_protocol(MYSQLND_MEMORY_POOL_CHUNK * row_buffer, zv
 	end_field = (start_field = fields) + field_count;
 
 	/* The status byte and the complete null bitmap must fit the row. */
-	size_t null_bytes = field_count / 8 + 1 + (field_count % 8 > 6);
+	null_bytes = field_count / 8 + 1 + (field_count % 8 > 6);
 	if (rbs == 0 || null_bytes > rbs - 1) {
 		php_error_docref(NULL TSRMLS_CC, E_WARNING, "Malformed server packet. Incomplete null bitmap");
 		DBG_RETURN(FAIL);
```

<details><summary>#326 3e136e0 Fix MySQLnd possible buffer over read in auth_protocol: VC11 compatibility</summary>

```diff
+++ backport/ext/mysqlnd/mysqlnd_wireprotocol.c
--- a/ext/mysqlnd/mysqlnd_wireprotocol.c
+++ b/ext/mysqlnd/mysqlnd_wireprotocol.c
@@ php_mysqlnd_greet_read(void * _packet, MYSQLND_CONN_DATA * conn TSRMLS_DC)
 	}
 
 	if (packet->server_capabilities & CLIENT_PLUGIN_AUTH) {
+		size_t remaining_size;
+
 		BAIL_IF_NO_MORE_DATA;
 		/* The server is 5.5.x and supports authentication plugins */
-		size_t remaining_size = packet->header.size - (size_t)(p - buf);
+		remaining_size = packet->header.size - (size_t)(p - buf);
 		if (remaining_size == 0) {
 			/* Might be better to fail but this will fail anyway */
 			packet->auth_protocol = estrdup("");
@@ php_mysqlnd_greet_read(void * _packet, MYSQLND_CONN_DATA * conn TSRMLS_DC)
 			/* Check if NUL present */
 			char *null_terminator = memchr(p, '\0', remaining_size);
 			size_t auth_protocol_len;
+			char *auth_protocol;
 			if (null_terminator) {
 				/* If present, do basically estrdup */
 				auth_protocol_len = null_terminator - (char *)p;
```

```diff
 	char *lc_line = lc_header_bag;
 
 	while (*lc_line != '\0') {
+		const char *lc_colon;
```

<details><summary>#330 2764f3c Fix bug #78793 for 5.6: maker note entries past a guessed offset</summary>

```diff
+++ backport/ext/exif/exif.c
--- a/ext/exif/exif.c
+++ b/ext/exif/exif.c
@@ static int exif_process_IFD_in_MAKERNOTE(image_info_type *ImageInfo, char * valu
 
 	for (de=0;de<NumDirEntries;de++) {
 		size_t offset = 2 + 12 * de;
+		if (offset > (size_t)data_len) {
+			/* MN_OFFSET_GUESS: the data ends before this entry, data_len - offset would wrap */
+			exif_error_docref("exif_read_data#error_ifd" EXIFERR_CC, ImageInfo, E_WARNING, "Illegal IFD size: 0x%04X > 0x%04X", (int)offset, data_len);
+			return FALSE;
+		}
 		if (!exif_process_IFD_TAG(ImageInfo, dir_start + offset,
 								  offset_base, data_len - offset, displacement, section_index, 0, maker_note->tag_table TSRMLS_CC)) {
 			return FALSE;
+++ backport/ext/exif/tests/bug78793_guess.phpt
new file mode 100644
--- /dev/null
+++ b/ext/exif/tests/bug78793_guess.phpt
@@
+--TEST--
+Bug #78793 backport: maker note entry past the data of a guessed offset (MN_OFFSET_GUESS)
+--SKIPIF--
+<?php if (!extension_loaded('exif')) print 'skip exif extension not available'; ?>
+--FILE--
+<?php
+/* Canon maker note of 10 entries (122 bytes). Entry 0 holds 76 where the guessing code reads
+ * the offset of the first value, so offset_diff is 50 and only 72 bytes of data remain:
+ * entries 6 to 9 start past them. Entry 9 asks for 64 bytes at 120 from the guessed base,
+ * past the end of the maker note. */
+$mn = pack('v', 10);
+for ($i = 0; $i < 9; $i++) {
+	$mn .= pack('vvVV', 0x100 + $i, 3, 1, $i ? 0 : 76);
+}
+$mn .= pack('vvVV', 0x109, 2, 64, 120);
+
+$tiff = "II*\0" . pack('V', 8)
+	. pack('v', 2) . pack('vvVV', 0x010F, 2, 6, 38) . pack('vvVV', 0x8769, 4, 1, 44) . pack('V', 0)
+	. "Canon\0"
+	. pack('v', 1) . pack('vvVV', 0x927C, 7, strlen($mn), 62) . pack('V', 0)
+	. $mn;
+$file = __DIR__ . '/bug78793_guess.tiff';
+file_put_contents($file, $tiff);
+
+$data = @exif_read_data($file, 'MAKERNOTE', true);
+var_dump(isset($data['MAKERNOTE']['UndefinedTag:0x0100']));
+var_dump(isset($data['MAKERNOTE']['UndefinedTag:0x0109']));
+?>
+--CLEAN--
+<?php @unlink(__DIR__ . '/bug78793_guess.tiff'); ?>
+--EXPECT--
+bool(true)
+bool(false)
```

</details>

```diff
@@ PHP_FUNCTION(imap_mail_compose)

 				disp_param = tmp_param = NULL;
 				while (zend_hash_get_current_data(Z_ARRVAL_PP(pvalue), (void **) &disp_data) == SUCCESS) {
+					zend_hash_get_current_key(Z_ARRVAL_PP(pvalue), &key, &ind, 0);
 					CHECK_HEADER_INJECTION(key, 0, "body disposition key");
 					disp_param = mail_newbody_parameter();
-					zend_hash_get_current_key(Z_ARRVAL_PP(pvalue), &key, &ind, 0);
 					disp_param->attribute = cpystr(key);
 					convert_to_string_ex(disp_data);
 					CHECK_HEADER_INJECTION(Z_STRVAL_PP(disp_data), 0, "body disposition value");
@@ PHP_FUNCTION(imap_mail_compose)
 			if (Z_TYPE_PP(pvalue) == IS_ARRAY) {
 				disp_param = tmp_param = NULL;
 				while (zend_hash_get_current_data(Z_ARRVAL_PP(pvalue), (void **) &disp_data) == SUCCESS) {
+					zend_hash_get_current_key(Z_ARRVAL_PP(pvalue), &key, &ind, 0);
 					CHECK_HEADER_INJECTION(key, 0, "body type.parameters key");
 					disp_param = mail_newbody_parameter();
-					zend_hash_get_current_key(Z_ARRVAL_PP(pvalue), &key, &ind, 0);
 					disp_param->attribute = cpystr(key);
 					convert_to_string_ex(disp_data);
 					CHECK_HEADER_INJECTION(Z_STRVAL_PP(disp_data), 0, "body type.parameters value");
+++ backport/ext/imap/tests/bug80710_3.phpt
new file mode 100644
--- /dev/null
+++ b/ext/imap/tests/bug80710_3.phpt
@@
+--TEST--
+Bug #80710 (imap_mail_compose() header injection) - parameter names of the top-level body
+--SKIPIF--
+<?php
+if (!extension_loaded("imap")) die("skip imap extension not available");
+?>
+--FILE--
+<?php
+$envelope = array("from" => "joe@example.com", "to" => "foo@example.com");
+$injected = "x\r\nX-Injected: 1\r\nfoo";
+
+function body($parameters, $disposition) {
+	$part = array("type" => TYPETEXT, "subtype" => "plain", "contents.data" => "text");
+	if ($parameters) {
+		$part["type.parameters"] = $parameters;
+	}
+	if ($disposition) {
+		$part["disposition.type"] = "attachment";
+		$part["disposition"] = $disposition;
+	}
+	return array($part);
+}
+
+/* the injected name comes last: it is the one the check used to miss */
+var_dump(imap_mail_compose($envelope, body(array("format" => "flowed", $injected => "bar"), null)));
+var_dump(imap_mail_compose($envelope, body(null, array("filename" => "a.txt", $injected => "bar"))));
+var_dump(is_string(imap_mail_compose($envelope, body(array("format" => "flowed"), array("filename" => "a.txt")))));
+?>
+--EXPECTF--
+Warning: imap_mail_compose(): header injection attempt in body disposition key in %s on line %d
+bool(false)
+
+Warning: imap_mail_compose(): header injection attempt in body type.parameters key in %s on line %d
+bool(false)
+bool(true)
```

</details>

```diff
new file mode 100644
--- /dev/null

@@
+--TEST--
+Phar::setSignatureAlgorithm() with Phar::OPENSSL signs every time (result of openssl_sign() read after it was freed)
+--SKIPIF--
+<?php
+if (!extension_loaded("phar")) die("skip");
+$arr = Phar::getSupportedSignatures();
+if (!in_array("OpenSSL", $arr)) die("skip openssl support required");
+?>
+--INI--
+phar.require_hash=0
+phar.readonly=0
+--FILE--
+<?php
+$fname = dirname(__FILE__) . '/' . basename(__FILE__, '.php') . '.phar';
+$private = openssl_get_privatekey(file_get_contents(dirname(__FILE__) . '/files/private.pem'));
+$pkey = '';
+openssl_pkey_export($private, $pkey, NULL, array('config' => dirname(__FILE__) . '/files/openssl.cnf'));
+$p = new Phar($fname);
+$p['file1.txt'] = 'hi';
+$failed = 0;
+for ($i = 0; $i < 100; $i++) {
+	try {
+		$p->setSignatureAlgorithm(Phar::OPENSSL, $pkey);
+		$sig = $p->getSignature();
+		if ($sig['hash_type'] !== 'OpenSSL') {
+			$failed++;

+		$failed++;
+	}
+}
+var_dump($failed);
+?>
+===DONE===
+--CLEAN--

+?>

 		case IS_BOOL:
-			efree(retval_ptr);
 			if (Z_BVAL_P(retval_ptr)) {

 				*signature = estrndup(Z_STRVAL_P(zsig), Z_STRLEN_P(zsig));
 				*signature_len = Z_STRLEN_P(zsig);
 				zval_dtor(zsig);
 				efree(zsig);
 				return SUCCESS;
```

<details><summary>#334 2530d40 Fix bug #78876 for 5.6: bound the length of a POST variable</summary>

```diff
+++ backport/main/rfc1867.c
--- a/main/rfc1867.c
+++ b/main/rfc1867.c
@@ static char *multipart_buffer_read_body(multipart_buffer *self, unsigned int *le
 {
 	char buf[FILLUNIT], *out=NULL;
 	unsigned int total_bytes=0, read_bytes=0;
+	int too_long = 0;
 
 	while((read_bytes = multipart_buffer_read(self, buf, sizeof(buf), NULL TSRMLS_CC))) {
+		if (too_long) {
+			continue;
+		}
+		/* PHP 5 strings hold at most INT_MAX bytes, and total_bytes + read_bytes + 1 must not wrap:
+		 * drop the value, but read the rest of the part so that the next one is parsed */
+		if (read_bytes > (unsigned int)INT_MAX - 1 - total_bytes) {
+			php_error_docref(NULL TSRMLS_CC, E_WARNING, "POST variable value too long, value discarded");
+			too_long = 1;
+			if (out) {
+				efree(out);
+				out = NULL;
+			}
+			total_bytes = 0;
+			continue;
+		}
 		out = erealloc(out, total_bytes + read_bytes + 1);
 		memcpy(out + total_bytes, buf, read_bytes);
 		total_bytes += read_bytes;
```

</details>
